<title>LucyKitchen — Factory Direct Wholesale</title>
<style>
  :root{--bg:#f7f8fa;--card:#fff;--ink:#222;--sub:#666;--brand:#e8542a;--line:#eee;
        --shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(20,30,50,.08);--green:#0a7d3b;--green-dark:#08632f;}
  *{box-sizing:border-box}
  body{margin:0;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",Roboto,"PingFang SC","Microsoft YaHei",sans-serif;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink)}
  .topbar{background:var(--green);color:#fff;font-size:14px;padding:8px 16px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:18px;align-items:center;position:sticky;top:0;z-index:100}
  .topbar a{color:#fff;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600}
  header{max-width:1280px;margin:0 auto;padding:18px 16px 6px}
  header h1{font-size:22px;margin:0}
  header p{margin:4px 0 0;font-size:13px;color:var(--sub)}
  .cats{max-width:1280px;margin:12px auto 0;padding:0 16px;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .cats button{border:1px solid var(--line);background:#fff;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 14px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;color:var(--sub);font-weight:600}
  .cats button.active{background:var(--brand);color:#fff;border-color:var(--brand)}
  .toolbar{max-width:1280px;margin:12px auto 0;padding:0 16px;display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .toolbar input{flex:1;min-width:220px;padding:11px 14px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;font-size:15px;outline:none;background:#fff}
  .count{font-size:13px;color:var(--sub);white-space:nowrap}
  .grid{max-width:1280px;margin:16px auto 40px;padding:0 16px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(190px,1fr));gap:16px}
  .card{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:var(--shadow);display:flex;flex-direction:column}
  .img-wrap{aspect-ratio:1/1;background:#f0f1f3;cursor:zoom-in;overflow:hidden}
  .img-wrap img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block;transition:transform .25s}
  .img-wrap:hover img{transform:scale(1.05)}
  .info{padding:10px 12px 12px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px}
  .sku{font-weight:800;font-size:14px;color:var(--brand)}
  .catname{font-size:12px;color:var(--sub)}
  .inquiry{margin-top:auto;display:inline-block;text-align:center;text-decoration:none;background:var(--brand);color:#fff;font-weight:700;font-size:13px;padding:9px 10px;border-radius:9px}
  .inquiry:hover{background:#cf431f}
  .more-wrap{text-align:center;margin:10px auto 50px}
  .more-btn{border:1px solid var(--brand);color:var(--brand);background:#fff;font-weight:700;padding:12px 28px;border-radius:999px;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px}
  .more-btn:hover{background:var(--brand);color:#fff}
  .empty{text-align:center;color:var(--sub);padding:40px}
  footer{background:#fff;border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding:28px 16px;text-align:center;font-size:14px;color:#444;line-height:2}
  footer a{color:var(--green);text-decoration:none}
  .lb{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(10,12,18,.86);display:none;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:50;padding:24px}
  .lb.open{display:flex}
  .lb img{max-width:92vw;max-height:82vh;border-radius:10px;background:#fff}
  .lb .cap{position:absolute;bottom:22px;left:0;right:0;text-align:center;color:#fff;font-size:15px}
  .lb .close{position:absolute;top:18px;right:22px;color:#fff;font-size:30px;cursor:pointer;line-height:1}
  @media(max-width:520px){.grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(140px,1fr));gap:10px}}
</style>
</head>

const CATS = [{"k":"Induction / Infrared / Gas Stove","n":"Induction / Infrared / Gas Stove"},{"k":"Air Fryer, Oven & Deep Fryer","n":"Air Fryer, Oven & Deep Fryer"},{"k":"Blender & Juicer Machine","n":"Blender & Juicer Machine"},{"k":"Coffee Machine & Coffee Pot","n":"Coffee Machine & Coffee Pot"},{"k":"Egg Beater","n":"Egg Beater"},{"k":"Pressure Cooker, Rice Cooker & Toaster","n":"Pressure Cooker, Rice Cooker & Toaster"},{"k":"Food Processor","n":"Food Processor"},{"k":"Coffee Grinder","n":"Coffee Grinder"},{"k":"Hair Dryer & Hair Straightener & Clipper","n":"Hair Dryer & Hair Straightener & Clipper"},{"k":"Handheld Iron & Garment Steamer","n":"Handheld Iron & Garment Steamer"},{"k":"Electric Iron","n":"Electric Iron"},{"k":"Juicer, Slow Juicer & Citrus Juicer","n":"Juicer, Slow Juicer & Citrus Juicer"},{"k":"Electric Kettle","n":"Electric Kettle"},{"k":"Meat Grinder & Stand Mixer","n":"Meat Grinder & Stand Mixer"},{"k":"Oil Heater, Fan Heater & Electric Fan","n":"Oil Heater, Fan Heater & Electric Fan"},{"k":"Sandwich Maker & Steak Grill","n":"Sandwich Maker & Steak Grill"},{"k":"Hand Blender (Stirring Rod)","n":"Hand Blender (Stirring Rod)"},{"k":"Water Dispenser, Ice Maker & Scale","n":"Water Dispenser, Ice Maker & Scale"}];
let cur = '', page = 1, PER = 60;
const WA = 'https://wa.me/8618105791688?text=';
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[m]))}
function buildCats(){
  var el = document.getElementById('cats');
  var h = '<button class="active" data-k="">All (2649)</button>';
  CATS.forEach(function(c){ h += '<button data-k="'+esc(c.k)+'">'+esc(c.n)+'</button>'; });
  el.innerHTML = h;
  el.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(function(b){
    b.addEventListener('click', function(){
      el.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(function(x){ x.classList.remove('active'); });
      b.classList.add('active');
      cur = b.getAttribute('data-k'); page = 1; render();
    });
  });
}
function filtered(){
  var q = document.getElementById('search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  return ITEMS.filter(function(it){
    if(cur && it.c !== cur) return false;
    if(q && it.n.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0 && it.f.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
    return true;
  });
}
function render(){
  var list = filtered();
  document.getElementById('count').textContent = list.length + ' products';
  var slice = list.slice(0, page*PER);
  var h = '';
  slice.forEach(function(it){
    var name = it.n;
    h += '<div class="card">'
      + '<div class="img-wrap" data-f="'+it.f+'" data-cs="'+esc(it.n)+'">'
      + '<img loading="lazy" src="images/'+it.f+'" alt="'+esc(it.n)+'">'
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="info">'
      + '<div class="sku">'+esc(it.n)+'</div>'
      + '<div class="catname">'+esc(it.cs)+'</div>'
      + '<a class="inquiry" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="'+WA+encodeURIComponent('Hello, I am interested in this product ('+it.n+' - '+it.cs+'). Please send me the price and MOQ.')+'">Inquiry</a>'
      + '</div></div>';
  });
  document.getElementById('grid').innerHTML = h || '<div class="empty">No products found</div>';
  document.getElementById('more').style.display = (slice.length < list.length) ? '' : 'none';
}
document.getElementById('grid').addEventListener('click', function(e){
  var w = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.img-wrap') : null;
  if(w) openLb(w.getAttribute('data-f'), w.getAttribute('data-cs'));
});
function showMore(){ page++; render(); }
function openLb(f,cap){
  document.getElementById('lbimg').src='images/'+f;
  document.getElementById('lbcap').textContent=cap;
  document.getElementById('lb').classList.add('open');
}
function closeLb(){ document.getElementById('lb').classList.remove('open'); }
buildCats(); render();
</script>
</body>
</html>
